# Card-effectcorpus 13101-13200

> Automatisch concept uit de geautoriseerde bronintentie. De oorspronkelijke kaarttekst is niet opgenomen. `displayText` en `effectSpecDraft` moeten voor runtime-implementatie per effectfamilie en kaart worden gevalideerd.

    "displayText":  "Availability or trigger: When this card is Normal Summoned. Resolution: The controller may excavate the top 3 cards of the controller\u0027s Deck, after that succeeds, The controller may banish 1 excavated monster to move 1 monster with its same name into the controller\u0027s hand, from either the controller\u0027s Deck or the other excavated cards, additionally shuffle the rest into the Deck. Availability or trigger: Once per turn, during the End Phase, if this card is in the controller\u0027s GY because it was sent there this turn after being Normal Summoned/Set.
